HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000065/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2d8f259e5d09d8314e3c","text":"1. **Return to the final gathering point.** Evidence: `ء و ب` B001 turning back to a return-place/gathering point. Structural cue: after divine punishment is named, the discourse shifts to first-person plural divine claim. Role: `إياب` gathers the trajectories of faces, effort, refusal, and signs back to their source. Abductive move: every movement shown earlier has a terminus. Reading change: the ayah reads as closure of motion, not merely future destination.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000065/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_780d015d4bd56abcadc1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:25:3\",\"root_000065\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Dönüş, yüz çevirenin yön değiştirme girişimini tersine çevirir; öne alınmış ilahi hedef, kaçışın yönsel imkânını kapatır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000065/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_84584486cf75e66ed55a","text":"4. **Bodily travel itself is gathered into the image of return.** **Lexical:** `ء و ب` B003, \"rhythmic return of limbs in travel.\" **Cue:** the window includes earth as extended surface in 88:20 and the bodily turn in 88:23 before naming final return. **Role:** B003 gives macro-direction a repeated bodily mechanism: every step advances through cycles of return within the limbs. **Abduction:** I infer that ordinary locomotion miniaturizes the final movement. **Reading change:** `إياب` can be felt not only as destination but as a return-pattern already embedded in travel itself.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000065/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8c0874dc79c5aacf701d","text":"2. **The repeated directional frame turns observers into the objects of direction.** **Lexical:** `ء و ب` B004, \"direction or quarter from which one comes,\" alongside B001. **Cue:** 88:20 directs human sight `إِلَى ٱلْأَرْضِ`; 88:25 replaces that outward object with first-person `إِلَيْنَا` and makes the people themselves return. **Role:** B004 gathers the `إلى` phrases into a directional system; B001 reverses the vector from gaze going outward to persons coming back. **Abduction:** I infer deliberate syntactic echo from the shared preposition. **Reading change:** the surah's observational geometry folds back on the observer: one who directs attention through the world is also moving toward an endpoint.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000065/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_914d6bb03cb954ba7c21","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:25:3\",\"root_000065\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Dönüşün yönü ve gelinen tarafı da belirtmesi, daha önce dış dünyaya yöneltilen bakışı gözleyenin kendi hareketine katlar: Dünyaya bakan kişi de belirlenmiş bir yöne gitmektedir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000065/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_91d0e6d96c142a756b2e","text":"3. **Return is the administrative handoff that makes account possible.** **Lexical:** `ء و ب` B001 includes gathering at a return-place. **Cue:** messenger-control is denied in 88:22, divine consequence is named in 88:24, and `ثُمَّ ... حِسَابَهُم` follows in 88:26. **Role:** gathering transfers dispersed persons into the jurisdiction where reckoning occurs. **Abduction:** I infer that return enables account; the packet gives order but does not describe a tribunal. **Reading change:** the ayah is procedural as well as spatial: return consolidates those whom no human reminder controls for the account claimed by God.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000065/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_92aea32724fa5c2234b1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:25:3\",\"root_000065\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yolculukta uzuvların ritmik geri gelişi, nihai dönüşü soyut bir hedef olmaktan çıkarıp her adımda küçük ölçekte tekrarlanan bedensel bir düzen gibi hissettirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000009/B009","root_000065/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b0ba97380bb452bcb475","text":"2. **Rhythmic return after creaturely travel.** Evidence: `ء و ب` B003 rhythmic return of limbs in travel/camel travel by day; earlier `ء ت ي` B009 camel foreleg return and `ء ب ل` B001 camels. Structural cue: camel and movement roots are activated before the final return. Role: `إياب` becomes embodied as repeated travel-motion, not only metaphysical return. Abductive move: the surah’s pastoral travel body supplies a model for return. Reading change: return reads as a gait-like inevitability: each stride comes back into account.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_cf5584d5733e58a3f79e","text":"The camel-travel branch made `إياب` less abstract and more kinetic. The earlier face/orientation language made the final `إلينا` feel like the correction of all misdirected looking and turning.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d2e2566a3ee67e7c8636","text":"88:26 revealed the purpose of return as accountable gathering. It also corrected a purely spatial reading of `إيابهم`: reaching the destination is an administrative transition into reckoning, not the endpoint of the mechanism.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000065/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_deb7a3e018c0c23a0655","text":"1. **Return reverses the rejecter's attempted change of direction.** **Lexical:** `ء و ب` B001, \"turning back to a return-place.\" **Cue:** `تَوَلَّىٰ` in 88:23 describes withdrawal, but emphatic `إِنَّ` and fronted `إِلَيْنَا` in 88:25 fix the endpoint before naming `إِيَابَهُمْ`. **Role:** turning away supplies outbound motion; return supplies reversal; fronting supplies the nonnegotiable destination. **Abduction:** I infer a direct reversal between the two roots across the intervening consequence. **Reading change:** `إيابهم` is not generic recurrence but the cancellation of directional escape.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000065/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e8196aba4c589c9c465a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:25:3\",\"root_000065\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Dönüşün aynı zamanda toplanma olması, dağılmış kişileri insan denetiminin dışında hesabın yapılacağı yetki alanında bir araya getiren idari geçiş kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000065/B004","root_001630/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f087091c439a1b9d92ed","text":"3. **Return from every direction faced.** Evidence: `ء و ب` B004 direction/quarter from which one comes; `و ج ه` B002 orientation, `و ل ي` B006/B007 turning toward/away. Structural cue: the surah repeatedly organizes faces and directions. Role: `إلينا` fixes the true direction after misorientation. Abductive move: all faced directions ultimately resolve into one return-direction. Reading change: the ayah corrects orientation: whatever one turned toward or away from, the return is “to Us.”","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
